Add routing for direct-XKB non-XKB-prefixed input methods.

NativeInputMethodEngineObserver (in CrOS Input Method Framework)
should route direct-XKB Thai input methods (vkd_th_tis & vkd_th,
formerly rule-based) to neither IME Service nor VK+IME extension.

These input methods should be handled directly & solely by system
XKB layouts, without ever connecting to IME Service or activating
VK+IME extension.

This new direct-XKB routing is functionally similar to that for
input methods with "xkb:" ID prefix, but the latter is
conditional on assistive features being disabled.

[dbsc] Restore the IdP attestation key for GenerateKey

Sessions loaded from disk hold only the wrapped AIK until first use, so
HandleGenerateKeyHeader() refused IdP sessions after a browser restart.

If the AIK isn't ready and there is a session store,
HandleGenerateKeyHeader() now restores it through the store, like the
service already does for the session binding key. When the restore
completes, it looks the session up again and writes the AIK back. A
refresh during the restore keeps the AIK state, so the request
continues with the refreshed session. If the IdP session was deleted or
replaced by a new registration while the restore was pending, the
request fails instead of using the new session's key.

SingleSignOnKeyManager::GenerateKey() no longer takes the AIK. It
returns a callback through which the service delivers the AIK or an
error, and it generates the RP key while the restore runs. The key is
certified once both are there, and deleted if the restore fails. The
key manager knows the request from the start, so clearing
pre-provisioned keys or cancelling the request stops it on the restore
path the same way as on the direct path. A clear only fails requests
whose RP origin it matches.

A permanent restore failure clears the session's AIK so later requests
don't retry. The next refresh saves the session without the wrapped
AIK, so from then on the session has no AIK until the IdP registers it
again.

For the AIK, the request fails with the first error that applies: a
deleted session reports kSigningKeyGenerationError, a failed restore
reports kFailedToUnwrapKey, and a session replaced by a new
registration reports kSigningKeyGenerationError. A failed key
generation or a clear can fail the request earlier, also with
kSigningKeyGenerationError.

The restore path charges the same single signing quota unit as the
direct path.

The knowledge base rule on revalidating sessions now notes that a
lookup by SessionKey after an asynchronous step can return a different
Session object.

[Blink] Fold FlushCanvas() into BaseRenderingContext2D

CanvasRenderingContext2D::FlushCanvas() and
OffscreenCanvasRenderingContext2D::FlushCanvas() both now simply
forward to BaseRenderingContext2D::FlushCanvasInternal().

Folds FlushCanvasInternal() directly into
BaseRenderingContext2D::FlushCanvas(), removes
OffscreenCanvasRenderingContext2D::FlushCanvas(), and replaces
CanvasRenderingContext2D::FlushCanvas() with an inline
CanvasHibernationHandler::Delegate forwarding override (matching
HasBacking(), IsBackingValid(), IsBackingAccelerated(), and Snapshot()).
BaseRenderingContext2D::FlushCanvas() remains non-final so
TestRenderingContext2D can retain its non-backing test override.

[Blink] Remove !canvas() check in CRC2D::FlushCanvas()

CanvasRenderingContext2D::FlushCanvas() guarded FlushCanvasInternal()
with if (!canvas()) return std::nullopt, unlike
OffscreenCanvasRenderingContext2D::FlushCanvas() which calls
FlushCanvasInternal() directly.

In production, canvas() becomes null only during Oilpan pre-finalization
of the mutually-referenced HTMLCanvasElement / CanvasRenderingContext2D
pair:
- BaseRenderingContext2D::Dispose() runs ResetRecorder() before clearing
  host_ [1], after which FlushCanvasInternal() already returns
  std::nullopt on !recorder [2].
- If HTMLCanvasElement::Dispose() calls DetachHost() first within the
  same atomic GC pause, it immediately clears context_ = nullptr without
  calling back into the context [3], and
  CanvasRenderingContext2D::Dispose() [4] ->
  BaseRenderingContext2D::Dispose() [1] runs synchronously before
  control returns to the event loop without invoking FlushCanvas() (both
  objects are already unreachable, so no JS, task observer, timer, IPC
  observer, hibernation callback, or other pre-finalizer can invoke
  FlushCanvas() in between).

Removing the !canvas() check makes
CanvasRenderingContext2D::FlushCanvas() identical to
OffscreenCanvasRenderingContext2D::FlushCanvas() in preparation for
folding FlushCanvas() into BaseRenderingContext2D.

[2]

[Blink] Enforce non-null Raster/SharedImageInterface in SharedGpuContext

Add CHECKs in SharedGpuContext::CreateContextProviderIfNeeded()
guaranteeing that any non-null context_provider_wrapper_ has a non-null
RasterInterface() and SharedImageInterface(), and document this
guarantee on ContextProviderWrapper() and
GetExistingContextProviderWrapper().

Why this holds in production:
- Every production SharedGpuContext context is created in
  CreateContextProviderIfNeeded() [1]:
  - On the main thread, it calls
    Platform::CreateSharedOffscreenGraphicsContext3DProvider() (sole
    production override: RendererBlinkPlatformImpl [2]), which wraps
    RenderThreadImpl::SharedMainThreadContextProvider() [3] in a
    WebGraphicsContext3DProviderImpl. SharedMainThreadContextProvider()
    creates a viz::ContextProviderCommandBuffer::CreateForRaster(),
    binds it via BindToCurrentSequence(), and returns nullptr unless
    binding returns kSuccess.
  - On worker threads, CreateContextProviderOnMainThread() [4] calls
    Platform::CreateRasterGraphicsContextProvider() (sole production
    override: RendererBlinkPlatformImpl [5]), which likewise wraps a
    viz::ContextProviderCommandBuffer::CreateForRaster() in a
    WebGraphicsContext3DProviderImpl. CreateContextProviderIfNeeded()
    then calls BindToCurrentSequence() on the worker thread and resets
    context_provider_wrapper_ to nullptr unless binding returns
    kSuccess [1].
- WebGraphicsContext3DProviderImpl::RasterInterface() [6] and
  SharedImageInterface() [7] delegate directly to the underlying
  ContextProviderCommandBuffer.
- Inside ContextProviderCommandBuffer::BindToCurrentSequence(), the
  kRaster branch unconditionally initializes raster_interface_ before
  returning kSuccess [8], and BindToCurrentSequence() unconditionally
  assigns shared_image_interface_ via
  channel_->CreateClientSharedImageInterface() [9], which
  unconditionally returns a non-null ClientSharedImageInterface [10].
- Neither raster_interface_ nor shared_image_interface_ is ever cleared
  after BindToCurrentSequence() succeeds (even on context loss).

All unit tests injecting into SharedGpuContext now also use raster
TestContextProviders (following deletion of FakeGLES2Interface and the
legacy GLES2 FakeWebGraphicsContext3DProvider constructor in the parent
CL).

[1] https://source.chromium.org/chromium/chromium/src/+/main:third_party/blink/renderer/platform/graphics/gpu/shared_gpu_context.cc;l=148-180;drc=1c6e60e79bebafc52177ba4e702fc4bc8e39ab6f
[2] https://source.chromium.org/chromium/chromium/src/+/main:content/renderer/renderer_blink_platform_impl.cc;l=843-865;drc=1c6e60e79bebafc52177ba4e702fc4bc8e39ab6f
[3] https://source.chromium.org/chromium/chromium/src/+/main:content/renderer/render_thread_impl.cc;l=1159-1175;drc=1c6e60e79bebafc52177ba4e702fc4bc8e39ab6f

967f2490c4a3ace65fcbbfa61582baa8f63b8f36 [ diff ] 
[Blink] Delete FakeGLES2Interface and GLES2 FakeWGC3DProvider ctor

With all SharedGpuContext tests now using raster TestContextProviders:
- Delete the unused fake_gles2_interface.h (and prune its BUILD.gn entry
  and two unused #includes).
- Remove the legacy GLES2 constructor and unused gl_ / raster_interface_
  members from FakeWebGraphicsContext3DProvider, simplifying
  InterfaceBase(), RasterInterface(), and IsContextLost().
- Remove the dead ContextGL() branch from
  SharedGpuContext::IsValidWithoutRestoringForTesting() (and the now
  unused gles2_interface.h include).

Why SharedGpuContext can never have a GL context (ContextGL() is always
nullptr):
- In production, SharedGpuContext only creates raster contexts via
  viz::ContextProviderCommandBuffer::CreateForRaster() on both the main
  thread (RenderThreadImpl::SharedMainThreadContextProvider() [1]) and
  worker threads
  (RendererBlinkPlatformImpl::CreateRasterGraphicsContextProvider()
  [2]). Raster contexts leave gles2_impl_ and trace_impl_ null, so
  ContextProviderCommandBuffer::ContextGL() [3] always returns nullptr.
- In tests, every remaining
  SharedGpuContext::SetContextProviderFactoryForTesting() caller either
  returns nullptr or goes through InitializeSharedGpuContext(), which
  injects a raster FakeWebGraphicsContext3DProvider whose ContextGL()
  returns nullptr.

[1]
https://source.chromium.org/chromium/chromium/src/+/main:content/renderer/render_thread_impl.cc;l=1159-1166;drc=1c6e60e79bebafc52177ba4e702fc4bc8e39ab6f
[2]
https://source.chromium.org/chromium/chromium/src/+/main:content/renderer/renderer_blink_platform_impl.cc;l=834-839;drc=1c6e60e79bebafc52177ba4e702fc4bc8e39ab6f
[3]
https://source.chromium.org/chromium/chromium/src/+/main:services/viz/public/cpp/gpu/context_provider_command_buffer.cc;l=447-456;drc=1c6e60e79bebafc52177ba4e702fc4bc8e39ab6f

[Blink] Migrate remaining FakeGLES2Interface test fixtures to TestContextProvider::CreateRaster()

Convert the last three test fixtures that inject the legacy GLES2-only
FakeWebGraphicsContext3DProvider(FakeGLES2Interface*) into
SharedGpuContext (leaving RasterInterface() and SharedImageInterface()
null) to use viz::TestContextProvider::CreateRaster() +
InitializeSharedGpuContext():
- OffscreenCanvasTest (offscreen_canvas_test.cc)
- SoftwareCompositingTest (shared_gpu_context_test.cc)
- ScrollingTestWithAcceleratedContext (scrolling_test.cc)

This matches production SharedGpuContext (which always creates raster
contexts) and allows deleting FakeGLES2Interface and enforcing non-null
RasterInterface() on SharedGpuContext::ContextProviderWrapper() in
follow-up CLs.

parent
73e9ad327531408e5e550753d0bf95ba4010b8c8 [ diff ] 
[Autofill] Reject PWM ML password predictions in Autocomplete

AutofillField::GetComputedPredictionResult() only surfaces on-device
Password Manager ML predictions for ONE_TIME_CODE, leaving password
classifications (such as ACCOUNT_CREATION_PASSWORD or
CONFIRMATION_PASSWORD) hidden from AutofillField::Type(). Meanwhile,
Password Manager's FormDataParser::ParseUsingModelPredictions() treats
those predictions as password fields even on text inputs.

Check HeuristicSource::kPasswordManagerMachineLearning in
IsPredictedFieldTypeSaveable() so ML-predicted password fields are not
persisted to Autocomplete history.

TAG=agy

[Autofill] Check all server predictions in IsPredictedFieldTypeSaveable

AutofillField::server_type() only inspects server_predictions_[0],
whereas Password Manager's GetServerType() scans all entries in
server_predictions() for password-related predictions. Extract
IsFieldTypeSaveable() and inspect every entry in
field->server_predictions() so secondary password or sensitive server
predictions are also excluded from Autocomplete history.

6b9ef4ae039e90e431adbcc2d4d1d0c320f835c7 [ diff ] 
[Autofill] Match password autocomplete tokens case-insensitively

FormFieldData::autocomplete_attribute() preserves the HTML attribute's
original casing from the renderer (GetAutocompleteAttribute()), whereas
IsHtmlFieldTypeSaveable() checked for "current-password" and
"new-password" case-sensitively. Lowercase the attribute with
base::ToLowerASCII() before matching so mixed/uppercase password
autocomplete tokens are excluded from Autocomplete history.

TAG=agy

[Public Transit] Wait for the shown controls offset in ToolbarFacility

ToolbarFacility, the default Facility of every CtaPageStation, now
also waits for BrowserControlsOffsetCondition.shown(): the browser
controls are fully shown at their current position, top or bottom.
Before, the toolbar views could be visible while the controls were
partly scrolled off.

This is a step towards replacing BrowserControlsFacility with
ToolbarFacility and Outcomes. Every Trip to a CtaPageStation now also
waits for the controls to be fully shown.

9a259d3f3df5ae02e4fc0a9818ad28ec35a0e5d6 [ diff ] 
Reland "views: Close menus when the owner's window is moved or resized"

This is a reland of commit 52a765738fd9f41f6b76ed83e40eb2d3e5ad1e87

The original change was reverted because
ExclusiveAccessBubbleViewsTest.MenuDuringBrowserFullscreenDoesNotReshowBubble
failed on linux-wayland-mutter-rel-tests. On Wayland, the fullscreen
state is reported before the compositor resizes the window, so the test
opened its menu before the resize, which now closes the menu as
intended. ExclusiveAccessTest::WaitAndVerifyFullscreenState() now also
waits for a fullscreen window to fill its display, so the test opens its
menu after the resize.

Original change's description:
> views: Close menus when the owner's window is moved or resized
>
> Menus are positioned relative to their owner widget when shown. When
> the window manager tiles the browser window (e.g. Super+Arrow on
> Linux), the owner's show state does not change, so the existing
> show-state handling from crbug.com/40914555 does not close the menu.
> The App Menu and context menus then stay at their old position,
> detached from the window.
>
> Observe the owner's primary window widget and close all menus when its
> bounds differ from those at the time the menu was shown. Using the
> primary window resolves bubbles (e.g. toasts) to the browser window,
> so bounds changes Chrome makes to the bubble itself are ignored, and
> the comparison ignores move notifications that do not change the
> bounds (seen on X11 after entering fullscreen).
>
> On ChromeOS, shelf context menus now also close when the shelf hides
> for a pinned window, since the hotseat widget moves offscreen.
>
> Fixed: 40282054
> Change-Id: I28c03a1ddc1d7052e8de91d206cc7f107ff21fd8

> Reviewed-by: Colin Blundell <blundell@chromium.org>
> Cr-Commit-Position: refs/heads/main@{#1754789}

Fixed: 40282054

Revert CHECK_GE back to DCHECK_GE in GestureEventData

Revert CHECK_GE in GestureEventData constructor back to DCHECK_GE. The
CHECK macro conversion triggered crashes when motion_event_id was
negative, so reverting to DCHECK_GE avoids crashing in production builds
until the issue can be further investigated.

This fix was generated by the Crash Autofix pipeline for crash report
crash/fbcbcc7208400000. Please see go/crash-autofix for more details.

Convert pending node creation CHECK_GT and CHECK in AXTree to DCHECK_GT and DCHECK

Revert CHECK_GT and CHECK back to DCHECK_GT and DCHECK for update_state
pending create node count in AXTree::CreateNode. The CHECK_GT and CHECK
can fail if the update state has a pending create node count of 0,
resulting in release build crashes.

This fix was generated by the Crash Autofix pipeline for crash report
crash/af48096888400000 (CHECK_GT). Please see go/crash-autofix for more
details.

[Omnibox] Add NavigationPredictor::kEnterKeyDown

OmniboxSearchPrefetchOnEnterKeyDown starts a navigation-likely
SearchPrefetch for the default match on Omnibox Enter key down on
Android. It reused onSuggestionTouchDown(), so SearchPrefetchService and
SearchPreloadService saw the Enter key down as
NavigationPredictor::kTouchDown.

NavigationPredictor describes which user action signaled the navigation
(kMouseDown, kUpOrDownArrowButton, kTouchDown). This CL adds
kEnterKeyDown at the same granularity and bundles the Java/JNI entry
point as AutocompleteController#onNavigationLikely() so the trigger can
be identified on the native side.

There is no behavior change:
- kEnterKeyDown keeps using kOmniboxTouchDownPredictor as
  PreloadingPredictor to preserve histogram continuity during launch
  and the DSEv2 rollout without overhauling PreloadingAttempt (now
  documented on kOmniboxTouchDownPredictor; crbug.com/532219992).
- It shares the touch down settings in SearchPrefetchService
  (IsTouchDownPrefetchEnabled(), ignoring saver modes) and in
  SearchPreloadService (on-press configuration, saver modes, ahead of
  imminent navigation).
- AutocompleteMediator keeps applying the same per-session checks and
  metrics to both triggers.

(Internal document for more context:
http://doc/1yDW_2U7h3dSRfmgNmj0ylGnxrkujUhQ_P22PgD8jO8w)

[CALI] Add SortedSet and View Tree multi-selection

This CL introduces multi-selection state (`ViewEngaged.sels`) backed by
a new `SortedSet`, and enables clicking View Rows in the View Tree to
toggle selection. Selected rows render with a red highlight (magenta
when also hovered), and collapsed ancestors show a left indicator bar
via `.hides-selection` (using the NVA map).

Details:
* style.css:
  * `:root`: Add `--color-markup`, `--bg-markup`,
    `--color-hover-markup`, and `--bg-hover-markup`.
  * `#div-view-tree .view-row`: Add `cursor: pointer`,
    `&.selected-view` (with `&.hovered-view`), and `&.hides-selection`
    (with `&.hides-hover`).
* math.js:
  * Add binary-search helper `upperBound(sortedArr, key)`.
  * Add `SortedSet` (`has()`, `findNext()`, `add()`, `remove()`,
    `clear()`, `visitSorted()`).
* model.js: `ViewEngaged`: Add `SortedSet sels` and clear it in
  `clear()`.
* tree_ui.js:
  * `TreeVis`:
    * CTOR / `clear()`: Track `Set<number> hidesSelection`.
    * Add `_updateHidesSelection()` using `nvaMap` to mark collapsed
      ancestors of selected Views.
    * `updateVisibility()`: Call `_updateHidesSelection()`.
    * Add `updateAllSelections()` to sync `.selected-view` and
      `.hides-selection`, also called at the end of `populateViewList()`.
  * `TreeController`:
    * CTOR: Accept `onToggleSelection` callback.
    * Add transacted `updateAllSelections()`.
    * `bindAll()`: Wire non-toggle row clicks to `onToggleSelection()`
      (and drop unused local `views`).
* app.js: `MainController`: Add `handleSelectionToggle()` and pass as
  `onToggleSelection` to `TreeController`.

[TrustedVault] Verify FetchKeys version ordering across platforms

Clarify in TrustedVaultClient::FetchKeys() that returned keys are
ordered by ascending version from oldest to newest, and add
std::ranges::is_sorted() CHECKs on StandaloneTrustedVaultClient,
TrustedVaultClientAndroid, and FakeTrustedVaultClient.

Because Android (TrustedVaultClient.java), iOS (IOSTrustedVaultClient),
and iOS WebView (WebViewTrustedVaultClient) delegate key management to
external libraries/providers, defensively sort returned keys by
ascending version there instead of assuming backend ordering, with unit
tests covering Android, iOS, and iOS WebView.

Guard EvictBackForwardCachedControllee() against re-entrancy

2b948c09cb1b2d80b503f86c40f093c506d07d66
parent
286ade086e36ada79b6a39d5957eaa8a6afabf84 [ diff ] 
Guard EvictBackForwardCachedControllee() against re-entrancy

ServiceWorkerVersion::EvictBackForwardCachedControllee() evicts the
controllee's page from the back/forward cache and then updates
controllee tracking on `this`. Eviction can synchronously run arbitrary
code: e.g. if the page is being restored, the restore's
NavigationRequest is reset, which can release the last reference to a
SiteInstance, end a renderer process, and start a prerender whose
navigation activates a waiting service worker. That re-entrantly calls
EvictBackForwardCachedControllees() on `this` and can drop the last
reference to `this`.

Keep `this` alive with a scoped_refptr while evicting (also in
EvictBackForwardCachedControllees()), and update
`controllees_to_be_evicted_` and `bfcached_controllee_map_` before
calling EvictFromBackForwardCache() so re-entrant eviction or controllee
destruction does not remove the controllee twice.

parent
cf426efb8fe53d7e78ff6fb306abece2769077e7 [ diff ] 
[sync] Wait for initial push connection in download status

On Desktop and ChromeOS, push invalidations are received over an
in-process network connection established after browser launch rather
than by a persistent OS service. On a warm browser restart, Sync loads
its cached registration token from disk immediately and considers
invalidations enabled before that connection has logged in and drained
any server-side updates queued while the browser was closed. Within the
periodic poll interval, Sync's download-status API can therefore
prematurely report a data type as up-to-date while offline-queued
invalidations are still pending on the server.

In this patch, Sync waits for `GCMDriver`'s initial connection readiness
signal before reporting data types as up-to-date via download status
(after draining `sync_task_runner_` so any invalidations delivered ahead
of readiness are reflected first), while keeping invalidation state
unchanged for sync scheduling.

There is intentionally no fallback timeout to report `kUpToDate` or
`kError` if the push connection cannot be established (e.g. when offline
or on networks that block push traffic while allowing HTTPS): reporting
`kUpToDate` would misrepresent freshness, whereas reporting `kError`
would cause callers to treat transient connectivity issues as permanent
data-clearing errors. In such cases `GetDownloadStatusFor()` remains in
`kWaitingForUpdates` (guarded by the `kGCMWaitForInitialConnectionReady`
kill switch), while mid-session reconnects and Android continue to rely
on the existing best-effort contract.

While doing this, the test-only startup workaround introduced in
https://crrev.com/c/8849082 for workspace desk sync tests is removed, as
those tests now exercise the production fix directly.

[CALI] Add HitTester and Screenshot hover routing to View Tree

This CL completes bidirectional hover by adding a spatial grid (`HitTester`) to resolve pointer coordinates on the Screenshot to a containing `ViewNode`, and routing hover events from `ScreenshotController` through `MainController.handleHover()` to sync both the Screenshot overlay and View Tree (including `.hides-hover` on collapsed ancestors). To prevent large containers or overlapping overlays from obscuring smaller Views, `HitTester` prioritizes the smallest-area containing View (breaking ties by later pre-order index).

Details:
* math.js: `Rectangle`: Add `area` getter and `containsInclExcl(pt)`.
* model.js:
  * `ViewNode`: Cache `area` from `rect.area`.
  * Add `HitTester`: Partitions the screen into `16x16` cells (skipping
    empty or offscreen rects) and resolves `query(wpt)` to the smallest
    containing View (tie-breaking by later pre-order index).
  * `MainModel`: Store `hitTester`, build in `load()`, reset in
    `unload()`, and expose `queryHit(wpt)`.
* screenshot_ui.js: `ScreenshotController`:
  * CTOR: Accept `onHover` callback.
  * `_clearHoverState()`: Dispatch `onHover(null)`.
  * Add `_refreshViewHighlight()` to query `model.queryHit()` and
    dispatch `onHover()`, called on `pointermove`.
* app.js: `MainController`: Pass `onHover` callback to
  `ScreenshotController`.

[CALI] Add Screenshot pointer coordinate and pixel color readout

This CL adds pointer tracking over the Screenshot (`ScreenshotPointer`),
switching `#div-screenshot-info` on hover to show the cursor's World
coordinates in `px` and `dp` along with the sampled pixel's `#RRGGBB`
color swatch.

Details:
* index.html: `#div-screenshot-info`: Add `.div-pointer-info` with
  `.sp-pointer-px`, `.sp-pointer-dp`, `.sp-color-preview`, and
  `.sp-color-hex`.
* style.css:
  * `#div-screenshot .screenshot-inner`: Hide by default (`display:
    none`) and show via `&.active` once a screenshot is loaded.
  * `#div-screenshot-info`: Show `.div-pointer-info` when
    `data-show="pointer"`, style coordinate / color-swatch spans, and
    scope `&.too-narrow` to `.div-size-info`.
* model.js: `VisOptions`: Add `s2wDevice(s)` to map Scaled UI pixel
  offsets to World pixel centers (`(s + 0.5) / scale`).
* screenshot_ui.js:
  * `SS_INFO_SHOW`: Add `POINTER`.
  * `ScreenshotInfoVis`:
    * CTOR: Cache pointer info and color swatch elements.
    * Add `_hex2()` and `_showPointer(pointer, imageData)` to display
      World coordinates and sampled RGB hex value.
    * `update(pointer, imageData)`: Show pointer info when provided,
      otherwise fall back to size info or clear.
  * Add `ScreenshotPointer` to track active pointer coordinates in World
    space via `s2wDevice()`.
  * `ScreenshotVis`: Toggle `.active` on `.screenshot-inner` and cache
    `screenshotImageData` in `setScreenshot()` (cleared in `clear()`).
  * `ScreenshotController`: Instantiate `pointer` and bind
    `pointermove` / `pointerleave` on `.screenshot-inner` (gated on
    `model.isLoaded`) to update `screenshotInfoVis`.

ff243c85c8cfe5472d5b4d322e3d66632e2f6f5b

tree
879119bf9334bb9ade71df12f5a8a535d69dc7b8
parent
699cbfe52ec1e07ed924c6654c224d391fd051dd [ diff ] 
Use User instead of Profile in cups_print_job_notification_utils

GetAppShortNameUTF16() only uses AppRegistryCache of AppServiceProxy.
This CL switches it to look up apps::AppService via
apps::AppServiceRegistry with the AccountId, instead of
AppServiceProxyFactory.

With that, the Profile in cups_print_job_notification_utils is used
only for the user's prefs and the user name, both of which can be
taken from user_manager::User. So this CL also changes
UpdateNotificationBodyMessage() and its helpers to take
user_manager::User (or AccountId) instead of Profile, and
CupsPrintJobNotification passes its User, which it already holds.

The user name in the CLIENT_UNAUTHORIZED message is now taken from
User::GetDisplayEmail() for users with a GAIA account, instead of
Profile::GetProfileUserName() (i.e. IdentityManager's primary
account). For other users (e.g. Kiosk, Managed Guest Session, Guest),
an empty string is used as before.

The AppService must be registered for the user, because the
notification is created only for the user session. Note that, for
Guest session, AppService is tied to its off-the-record Profile, but it
is registered with the Guest user's AccountId. So now the app name is
also shown for Guest session.

In the unittest, AppServiceRegistry is instantiated, so that
AppServiceProxy for the Profile created by UserSessionTestEnvironment
is registered.

Bug: 477191550
Test: unit_tests --gtest_filter='*CupsPrintJob*:*PrintJobHistoryService*:*PrintingManager*'
Test: browser_tests --gtest_filter='*CupsPrint*:*PrintJob*:*PrintManagement*'

[Autofill] Fix invalid frame tree in OtpTargetFieldIdentifierTest

OtpTargetFieldIdentifierTest created two unparented drivers instead of
activating a primary main frame with a child frame.

This CL parents the second driver to the primary main frame and splits
FrameLifecycleAndRegistration into InactiveFrameIsIgnored,
EmbeddedFrameIsIgnored, and ChildFrameLifecycleAndRegistration so each
test keeps a valid frame tree (avoiding deactivating a parent while its
child is active or marking a child of a non-embedded parent as
embedded).

wm: Add kWindowHighlightBorderInsetsKey for DecorationController

Add `wm::kWindowHighlightBorderInsetsKey` (defaulting to
`ui::decoration::HighlightBorder::InsetsType::kNoInsets`) so windows
using `wm::kWindowHasHighlightBorderKey` can configure how their
highlight border is positioned relative to the window bounds.

Observe `kWindowHighlightBorderInsetsKey` in `wm::DecorationController`
and propagate its value when creating or updating a window's
`ui::decoration::HighlightBorder`.

parent
8846c1f091b56bca936aab4a5c6d5dddd6278fdf [ diff ] 
[chromium-bidi] Emit goog:session.changed on embedder ACL updates

- Emit `goog:session.changed` to restricted sessions whenever the
  embedder pushes an ACL update via `aclHandler.setOnAclChanged`.
- Clients in restricted sessions must learn immediately when the user
  or browser revokes or changes permissions while the connection stays
  open, without requiring `session.subscribe` or an ACL grant.
- Extend `aclHandler` with an optional `setOnAclChanged` registration
  callback, apply embedder updates immediately without waiting on
  pending `goog:session.setAcl` calls, and normalize the stored ACL.
- Omit `paused` from `goog:session.changed` for now while pause
  semantics are still being finalized; closing a navigable with a
  `perNavigable` entry remains silent.

author

Fri Nov 27 07:10:05 2026
tree
45bbd71659fbdbf92e089ba5e1b56f46d4de0418
parent
77247bae1e4c7507e7ea2670d6857d45a36a881e [ diff ] 
Shut down power globals in DemoModeSessionLengthLimiterTest

DemoModeSessionLengthLimiterTest initialized the fake
PowerManagerClient and PowerPolicyController in SetUp() but never shut
them down. A later AshTestHelper-based test in the same process then
skipped creating its own instances, but still shut down
PowerManagerClient in TearDown(), leaving the leaked
PowerPolicyController with a dangling raw_ptr to it. The dangling
raw_ptr check then crashed the following test, e.g. after
MagicBoostStateTest.IsUserEligibleForGenAIFeatures_DefaultAndWithMahiEnabled,
depending on test batching.

Shut both down in TearDown(), mirroring SetUp().

TAG=agy

Bug: None
Test: unit_tests --single-process-tests --gtest_filter='DemoModeSessionLengthLimiterTest.DemoModeForceNotWaitUserActivity:MagicBoostStateTest.IsUserEligibleForGenAIFeatures_DefaultAndWithMahiEnabled:MahiAvailabilityTest.MinorRestrictedUserCannotUseMahi'

bee0245bedeea4ca207824522090805b85bc77e4
parent
342d8f67bee16d6b37cb2fdaaf7fd9cd8dc89d28 [ diff ] 
session_manager: Split UserSessionTestEnvironment::LogIn()

Split UserSessionTestEnvironment::LogIn() into CreateSession() and
StartSession(), so that tests can observe the state between the Profile
creation and SessionManager::SessionStarted(), which corresponds to the
period such as onboarding in production. Some ash tests need it, e.g. the
ones using SimulateUserLogin() with `activate_session` false.

- CreateSession() calls SessionManager::CreateSession() and
  Delegate::CreateProfile(), if Delegate is set.
- StartSession() calls SessionManager::SessionStarted() for the pending
  session.
- LogIn() is kept as a shorthand of the two, so existing callers do not
  need any changes.

At most one session can be pending at a time, which is enforced by CHECKs.

Bug: 473653626
Test: components_unittests --gtest_filter=FakeUserSessionTestEnvironmentDelegate*
Test: unit_tests --gtest_filter=ChromeUserSessionTestEnvironmentDelegateTest.*:DemoSession*

[Public Transit] Wait for the strip scroll in TabStripRenderTest

In testFilledStrip_withPinnedAndUnpinnedTabs, selecting tab 5 starts a
strip scroll. CompositorAnimationHandler's testing mode doesn't finish
scrolls (finishAnimations() doesn't stop them either), so the halfway
scroll offset set right after it could be overridden when the scroll
ends.

Pinning and selecting is now the trigger of a Trip that reaches
TabStripFacility#settled(). The test scrolls halfway only after that.

Rename the golden ID to
tab_strip_filled_pinned_and_unpinned_scrolled_halfway so the old image
(where the selection scroll overwrote the halfway offset, leaving no
right fade) does not stay a positive.

AppHeaderCoordinatorBrowserTest from DOMUtils#clickNode() to
WebPageFacility and HtmlElement#clickTo().

Clicking the editable input now enters a SoftKeyboardFacility, keeping
each test's named 10 s keyboard timeout on the Trip. Timeouts now throw
TravelException instead of CriteriaHelper.TimeoutException.

Note: AppHeaderCoordinatorBrowserTest's two keyboard tests are currently
skipped/disabled by existing annotations (conflicting class/method
@Restriction and @DisabledTest). Verified on android-15-tablet-x64-rel
with annotations and the tablet filter bypassed: RootViewNotPadded
passes 20/20 (same as main), and RootViewPadded gets past
loadEditablePageAndShowKeyboard() 5/5 before hitting the same
pre-existing paddingBottom failure as main.

a8533341043bc6c7bf6b6cc51c224ce994e97df4 [ diff ] 
Enforce PartitionRootLock and atomic raw_size on SlotSpanMetadata

SlotSpanMetadata, SubsequentPageMetadata, and PartitionBucket mutate
per-span and per-bucket allocator state that must be synchronized with
PartitionRootLock(). Only Free() and AppendFreeList() carried Clang
thread-safety annotations, leaving other mutators and background readers
without compile-time lock checks. In addition, SubsequentPageMetadata
stored raw_size as a plain size_t even though live-slot queries read
GetUtilizedSlotSize() without holding PartitionRootLock(), and
SlotSpanMetadata::can_store_raw_size_ shared a bitfield word with
marked_full.

To enforce synchronization at compile time and eliminate metadata data
races:
- Add PA_EXCLUSIVE_LOCKS_REQUIRED(PartitionRootLock(root)) and runtime
  lock assertions to SlotSpanMetadata and PartitionBucket mutators.
- Make SubsequentPageMetadata::raw_size a private std::atomic<size_t>,
  and split GetRawSize(root) (locked, for PartitionPurgeSlotSpan())
  from GetRawSizeUnsynchronized() (relaxed load, for live-slot queries).
- Move SlotSpanMetadata::can_store_raw_size_ out of the mutable bitfield
  into a standalone const bool byte using PartitionPageMetadata's spare
  byte, removing the TSan suppression in CanStoreRawSize().

TAG=agy
CONV=ac616c71-05c2-4d85-85f1-1896341253d3

[viz] Submit GrDirectContext in FlushAndSubmitCopyOutput() under DrDC

CopyOutputRGBAInTexture() and CopyOutputNV12() render into a
destination SharedImage on the display compositor GPU thread and call
FlushAndSubmitCopyOutput() before ending write access and sending
CopyOutputSharedImageResult to the caller.

For Ganesh, FlushAndSubmitCopyOutput() only calls
GrDirectContext::submit() when end_semaphores is non-empty, whereas
thread-safe SharedImages backed by WrappedSkImageBacking under DrDC do
not produce semaphores.

Submit GrDirectContext in FlushAndSubmitCopyOutput() when DrDC is
enabled or when CPU sync is required, matching
SharedContextState::SubmitIfNecessary().

TAG=agy
CONV=ec124c89-c25c-47bb-ae7a-8d782a1d52ad

tree
75b8d3bed1a4b2afbc19926d4f7b4de7f56f558b
parent
aeac19c6b8e43e052398018ba8a0dcf056f59693 [ diff ] 
Synchronize single-slot normal-bucket raw_size updates in Realloc()

Single-slot normal-bucket spans store a non-atomic raw_size in
SubsequentPageMetadata that PartitionPurgeSlotSpan() reads under
PartitionRootLock().

PartitionRoot::ReallocInPlaceOrMove() releases PartitionRootLock()
before calling TryReallocInPlaceForNormalBuckets() so small-bucket
reallocations and GetOrCreateThreadCache() run unlocked. However, for
spans where CanStoreRawSize() is true,
TryReallocInPlaceForNormalBuckets() updated SetRawSize() without
re-acquiring PartitionRootLock(), racing with concurrent PurgeMemory()
calls reading a stale smaller raw_size and discarding pages from an
expanding allocation.

To synchronize raw_size updates with purging:
- Acquire PartitionRootLock() around GetSlotUsableSize() and
  SetRawSize() when CanStoreRawSize() is true in
  TryReallocInPlaceForNormalBuckets(), before GetOrCreateThreadCache().
- Assert DCheckRootLockIsAcquired() in SlotSpanMetadata::SetRawSize().
- Add unit tests covering single-slot normal-bucket in-place growth,
  shrink, and concurrent PurgeMemory().

TAG=agy
CONV=ac616c71-05c2-4d85-85f1-1896341253d3

bf0cb6856bfa0b12ebcb537ce470b9a080c9281e [ diff ] 
Remove obsolete TODOs & redundant comments in OnActivate().

The TODOs all refer to closed bugs, and they're:

- vague & obsolete (b:147709499): IME Service lifecycle looks
  vastly different than when this was added. Mojo resets appear
  sufficient as a standard way to disconnect. It's unclear in
  what "better way" the cleanup could be done.

- no longer feasible (b:251679480): Flag has been dismantled, but
  the flow cannot be merged with the "without VK+IME extension"
  case, as it explicitly needs to activate the extension.

- obsolete & unideal (b:181077907): IME Service still doesn't
  have self-shutdown capability. It doesn't sound ideal to always
  activate IME Service, only to expect it to self-shutdown based
  on info already available at activation time.

Those other inline comments look redundant, as they appear to
merely repeat what the self-explanatory code says.

Bug: b:589462840
Bug: b:147709499
Bug: b:251679480
Bug: b:181077907

This is currently a misleading and confusing misnomer, as the
routing & handling logic driven by the predicate:

- also covers (permanently & conditionally) direct-XKB input
  methods that don't connect to CrOS 1P IME Service (aka. "Native
  Mojo Engine"). Those are handled directly by system XKB while
  keeping CrOS 1P VK+IME extension dormant.

- doesn't exhaustively cover all input methods that do connect to
  the IME Service either: specifically, rule-based & Vietnamese
  input methods connect to the IME Service while also activating
  the VK+IME extension.

The common attribute across all cases where this predicate
returns true is that routing & handling do not involve activating
the VK+IME extension.

Without this rename, the situation could worsen as certain
rule-based input methods are migrated to dirext-XKB (e.g.
b:565986383, b:565990195).

build: Enable Armv8.2-A + LSE/RCPC/DotProd/FP16 on Android Desktop arm64

Android Desktop arm64 builds currently default to Armv8.0-A with
-moutline-atomics. Enable Armv8.2-A with LSE, LRCPC, DotProd, and FP16
for Clang and Rust when is_desktop_android && current_cpu == "arm64",
emitting inline LSE atomic instructions instead of out-of-line helpers.

Pass Clang features via -Xclang -target-feature after asmflags = cflags
instead of -march=, so per-target -march= flags in third_party targets
do not drop them and -Xclang is not passed to the assembler.

[Public Transit] Use WebPageFacility in Custom Tab tests

Add a CctTransitTestRule#startCustomTabActivityWithIntent() overload
that takes a CctPageStation builder, so Custom Tab tests can enter a
WebPageFacility in the same Trip as the CctPageStation.

Migrate TabReparentingTest and CustomTabActivityEphemeralTest from
CustomTabActivityTestRule + DOMUtils#clickNode() to CctTransitTestRule,
WebPageFacility and HtmlElement#clickTo():
- TabReparentingTest: click #select in a Trip that waits for the select
  popup, before and after "Open in browser" moves the tab to a
  WebPageStation in a new task (instead of an ActivityMonitor and
  CriteriaHelper polls).
- CustomTabActivityEphemeralTest: click #link_with_bounds in a Trip
  that arrives at a new CctPageStation (instead of
  CriteriaHelper.pollUiThread), read tabs via CctPageStation#getTab()
  and finish with CctPageStation#finishActivity().

TabReparentingTest#testTabReparentingBasic is @DisabledTest
(crbug.com/40904413); it only changes because it shares
reparentAndVerifyTab(). Timeouts now throw TravelException instead of
CriteriaHelper.TimeoutException, java.util.concurrent.TimeoutException,
or AssertionError.

c61bb7a1c9a7d84194fab20819c357a59e18061f [ diff ] 
file_manager: Move User-based path functions to //chromeos

This CL moves the following functions taking user_manager::User, and the
folder name constants they need, from
//chrome/browser/ash/file_manager/path_util.{h,cc} to
//chromeos/ash/components/file_manager/path_util.{h,cc} as they are:
- GetDefaultDownloadDirectoryForLinuxChromeOS()
- GetDownloadsFolder(const User&)
- GetMyFilesFolder(const User&)
- GetDownloadsMountPointName(const User&)
- GetShareCacheMountPointName(const User&)
- kFolderNameDownloads, kFolderNameMyFiles, kFolderNameShareCache

Their internal helper functions are moved together. Among them,
GetOverriddenMyFilesFolder() and GetOverriddenDownloadsFolder() are
exposed, with a comment that they are only for the deprecated Profile*
variants, which stay in //chrome.

The tests for them are moved to
chromeos/ash/components/file_manager/path_util_unittest.cc.

GetCrostiniMountPointName() and GetGuestOsMountPointName() stay in
//chrome, because they depend on guest_os::GuestId and crostini
constants.

chrome/browser/ash/file_manager/path_util.h includes the new header for
now, so that the existing callers keep working. They will be migrated
to include the new header directly later.

This allows code under //chromeos, and code that //chrome/browser/ash/
file_manager depends on (e.g. //chrome/browser/chromeos/policy/dlp), to
get the user's MyFiles/Downloads folders without a dependency cycle.

Compute address hash before delete in AllocatorShimTest

NewAndDeleteSingle(), NewAndDeleteTriplet(), and
InstallDispatchToPartitionAllocWithAdvancedChecks pass `new_ptr` and
`alloc_ptr` to Hash() after `delete` / `delete[]`, triggering
`-Wlifetime-safety-use-after-free`.

Compute Hash() before deleting the allocation, matching
MakeUniquePtrSingle() and MakeUniquePtrTriplet() in the same test file.

TAG=agy
CONV=6d6df2df-016f-4d0f-a91e-0347f62bc2bb

Use RunLoop::QuitClosure() in CookieStoreWorkerTestHelper
📋 Metadata

58399058709e5415517890950cdd1f86a8621d5f
[ log ]
author

tree
5601fc68984138424356c27cf348eaf6c1b08eb6
parent
d09f6b324b80397b1ff9172a9ac22fac1803d222 [ diff ] 
Use RunLoop::QuitClosure() in CookieStoreWorkerTestHelper

WaitForActivateEvent() stores the address of a stack-allocated
base::RunLoop in `quit_on_activate_`, relying on DispatchActivateEvent()
to clear it before `run_loop.Run()` returns. Clang's `-Wlifetime-safety`
intra-procedural analysis cannot see across callbacks and reports
`-Wlifetime-safety-dangling-field` (and `quit_on_activate_` would dangle
if `run_loop.Run()` timed out without DispatchActivateEvent() running).

Store `run_loop.QuitClosure()` in a `base::OnceClosure` instead of
holding a raw pointer to the stack `RunLoop`.

TAG=agy
CONV=6d6df2df-016f-4d0f-a91e-0347f62bc2bb

32483e23c2e7a64e835c0a8a80e4e4a73b3e779e
parent
201bd27479b4a31ec87cbdf7386420916842729e [ diff ] 
Move flat_tree internals out of base::internal

The Google C++ style guide advises against generic internal namespaces,
and helpers in base/containers/flat_tree.h (and its sibling
flat-container headers) are widely included across Chromium.

Move these implementation details into specific *_internal namespaces
matching existing base::flat_map_internal and base::to_array_internal
conventions:

- flat_tree.h (and flat_set.h's flat_tree deduction guides and
  flat_tree_unittest.cc): base::flat_tree_internal
- flat_map.h (GetFirst): existing base::flat_map_internal
- fixed_flat_map.h: base::fixed_flat_map_internal
- fixed_flat_set.h: base::fixed_flat_set_internal

Also update references to base::internal::flat_tree in raw_ptr_plugin,
manual-fields-to-ignore.txt, gdb_chrome.py, and chrome.natvis.

AutoResetCtaTransitTestRule was the only caller of

into AutoResetCtaTransitTestRule lets it manage night-mode relaunches
directly, while moving live-Activity UI/tab cleanup into a
package-private CtaStateResetter helper.

8b5a71dc42889ab95c1c511688831513e7ee60ad [ diff ] 
[a11y-image-desc] Relax Manta image size policy for icons and OCR

Alternate routing currently drops images outside `IsWithinDescPolicy`
before sending requests, blocking small UI icons and smaller or wide
OCR-only images from reaching the server.

- Add `IsWithinOcrPolicy` (`>= 16x16` px and aspect ratio `<= 200:1`)
  and drop images locally only when outside description, icon, and OCR
  policies.
- Remove `CHECK(request_info.desc_requested)` in
  `SendRequestBatchToServer` and report `ImageRequestIncludesIcon`
  alongside `ImageRequestIncludesDesc` (including on policy skips).
- Update and add unit tests for policy boundaries, wide OCR-only images,
  and mixed-size batches.

b7b6379fae2ca029b4bed28264f1df35b55531f1
parent
a548918a83328a776723214ed1e73a95c8fb8945 [ diff ] 
file_manager: Make the User-based path functions take const User&

This is a preparation to move the User-based path functions in
chrome/browser/ash/file_manager/path_util.cc to
//chromeos/ash/components/file_manager.

This CL makes the internal helper functions used by them take
const user_manager::User& instead of a nullable pointer:
- ShouldMountPrimaryUserDownloads()
- GetMountPointName()
- GetOverriddenMyFilesFolder()
- GetOverriddenDownloadsFolder()

The deprecated Profile* variants handle Profiles not associated with
any user by themselves instead. In production, such Profiles are Ash
internal Profiles, such as the sign-in Profile, and the callers filter
them out, so the fallbacks for them are reachable only in tests. They
are now guarded by CHECK_IS_TEST(base::NotFatalUntil::M163), with a
TODO to fix the tests.

Also, ShouldMountPrimaryUserDownloads() uses
SessionManager::GetPrimarySession() instead of the deprecated
UserManager::GetPrimaryUser(). As it takes a User now, it no longer
treats a Profile without a user as the primary user's one before
log-in on linux-chromeos. This is not reachable in production, as
described above.

The tests are updated accordingly. The tests for the User variants use
UserSessionTestEnvironment, so that the primary session is set up.

637b567065a4dd8fcb146b7e12d06a30a5ec1252 [ diff ] 
[iOS] Convert `AccountSwitchingPromoSceneAgent` to a profile agent

In https://crrev.com/c/8666319, `AccountSwitchingPromoSceneAgent` was
added as an `ObservingSceneAgent` with a per-scene
`_promoEvaluatedAtStartup` flag. On iPad, opening a second window
creates a second `SceneState` with its own agent instance, which
re-evaluates startup eligibility, emits `kEligibleOnStartup`, and calls
`RegisterPromoForSingleDisplay()` again.

Convert `AccountSwitchingPromoSceneAgent` into
`AccountSwitchingPromoProfileAgent`, a `SceneObservingProfileAgent`
attached once per profile in `ProfileController` when
`kEnableAccountPreviewSwitchingAccount` is enabled, so startup promo
evaluation runs once per profile rather than once per scene. Update
`BrowserModalHost` to retrieve it via `agentFromProfile:`.

Like other profile agents, the agent is created with `-init`, accesses
the profile through `self.profileState.profile`, and gets services from
it when needed. Once the profile is loaded, it observes it only if it is
the personal profile and has an `AccountPreviewDataService`. It stops
observing when the profile is destroyed (via
`RegisterProfileDestroyedCallback()`) or when removed from its
`ProfileState`, and ignores observer events after `-[ProfileController
shutdown]` clears `_state.profile`. `targetIdentity` returns `nil`
unless the target account is known not to be managed (since switching to
a managed account would trigger an unsupported profile switch).

Store the startup-evaluation flag in a file-scoped
`gPromoAlreadyEvaluated` global set only by the personal profile (never
by managed profiles), so switching from the personal profile to a
managed profile and back in the same Chrome session does not trigger a
second promo evaluation. Expose `+resetPromoAlreadyEvaluatedForTesting`
to clear it between unit tests.

One-pager view of the architecture:
http://go/velcro-bling-account-switch-dd

Test: ios_chrome_unittests --gtest_filter=*AccountSwitchingPromo*
Bug: 571565524

parent
1cdebbc5c66fae1ff6170a0c760153924d219a65 [ diff ] 
checkdeps: Factor out directory listing for parallel checking

Move the per-directory listing into _ScanDir() and the git directory
lookup into _EnsureGitSourceDirectories(), so that the parallel check
in the next CL can reuse them.

_ScanDir() uses os.scandir, which saves a stat() per entry:
chrome/browser 8.4s -> 8.1s in a single process, and about 0.7s off a
full-repo run with 8 workers.

Revert CHECK to DCHECK in GlobalAcceleratorListenerMac::OnHotKeyEvent

In GlobalAcceleratorListenerMac::OnHotKeyEvent, if an unexpected hotkey
event is received in release builds, id_accelerators_ does not contain
hot_key_id.id, causing the CHECK macro to fail and crash the process.

This change reverts the CHECK back to a DCHECK with a CHECK-exclusion
annotation to prevent crashes when unexpected hotkey events occur in
production.

This fix was generated by the Crash Autofix pipeline for crash report
crash/f116c71a08400000. Please see go/crash-autofix for more details.

Roll src/third_party/webpagereplay/ c97b499f0..9104210ce (5 commits)

https://chromium.googlesource.com/webpagereplay.git/+log/c97b499f01fe..9104210ce369

Rolled commits:
- 9104210ce369 Add opt-in deterministic crypto.randomUUID() to deterministic.js
- 40e5c5a4aef8 Add watchlist for victorvianna@
- 76762e25b9e8 Switch vpython3 to Python 3.14
- b6790a86142e Switch Python formatter from yapf to ruff
- c75f52f91320 Require archived requests to match the request's method

>>>>>>>>>>>>>>> Changes made by Chakra (AI agent) >>>>>>>>>>>>>>>
This fix was generated by Chakra, an AI agent that fixes broken
third-party rolls. Review it like any other change.

Upstream commit responsible for the failure:
- c75f52f913209db74a381b380c07d5fe221d884d
  ("Require archived requests to match the request's method")
  https://chromium-review.googlesource.com/c/webpagereplay/+/8773090

Reason for failure:
- Before c75f52f91320, Archive.findBestMatchInArchivedRequestSet in
  third_party/webpagereplay/src/webpagereplay/archive.go had a
  single-entry fast path that returned a URL's sole archived response
  regardless of HTTP method.
- Commit c75f52f91320 removed that fallback so Archive.FindRequest now
  requires the HTTP method to match.
- In //chrome/test:captured_sites_interactive_tests, TestRecipeReplayer
  explicitly navigates via HTTP GET to each recipe's "startingURL"
  (InitializeBrowserToExecuteRecipe) and to "loadPage" actions with
  "force": true (ExecuteForceLoadPage).
- Several historical .wpr archives (1 test on
  linux-password-manager-captured-sites-rel and 8 tests on
  linux-autofill-captured-sites-rel: sign_up_fill_naver,
  dicks_sporting_goods, hottopic, joann, kmart, pac_sun, pandora,
  payless, tickets) only recorded a POST entry for those target URLs
  because the page was reached via form submission during recording.
  Under strict method matching, those GET navigations received 404s and
  the tests timed out.

Fix (chrome/browser/autofill/captured_sites_test_utils.{h,cc}):
- Added PrepareReplayArchive(), invoked from
  WebPageReplayServerWrapper::Start() in replay mode, to preprocess the
  .wpr archive into a temporary directory (replay_archive_temp_dir_,
  cleaned up in WebPageReplayServerWrapper::Stop()):
  - Reads the companion .test recipe to collect only explicit GET
    navigation targets ("startingURL" and "force": true "loadPage"
    action URLs, stripping "#" fragments).
  - Inspects only those target URLs in the archive's "Requests" map; if
    a target URL lacks a GET entry, clones only its first valid non-HEAD
    entry with the request-line method rewritten to GET (skipping HEAD
    entries so responses with non-zero Content-Length and empty bodies
    are not turned into GET entries that fail WPR's script-injection
    re-serialization with unexpected EOF).
  - Re-serializes JSON with OPTIONS_OMIT_DOUBLE_TYPE_PRESERVATION to
    preserve 64-bit integer timestamps expected by Go's json.Unmarshal.
  - Returns the original .wpr path unchanged when no target URL needs
    modification.

Revert CHECK_LE to DCHECK_LE in View::AddChildViewAtImpl

Change the out-of-bounds check in View::AddChildViewAtImpl from CHECK_LE
back to DCHECK_LE. The CHECK macro caused crashes in release builds when
index was out of bounds of children_.size(). Reverting to DCHECK_LE
prevents release crashes while maintaining debug assertion behavior.

This fix was generated by the Crash Autofix pipeline for crash report
crash/1c4e84c488400000. Please see go/crash-autofix for more details.

fcb44f9ed5cab6a3f0192a6aa4f366e6b4442b05 [ diff ] 
[network] Stage NetToMojoPendingBuffer writes in a heap buffer

Callers of NetToMojoPendingBuffer and DataPipeProducer (when using a
FilteredDataSource filter) pass the two-phase write span into readers,
filters, and async cache writers that expect exclusive access to the
buffer rather than a live data-pipe ring buffer.

Stage NetToMojoPendingBuffer writes through a ref-counted heap
StagingBuffer (gated by kStageNetToMojoBuffer) and copy committed bytes
into the data pipe on Complete():
- Hold a StagingBuffer reference (instead of NetToMojoPendingBuffer) in
  NetToMojoIOBuffer when staged so async writers stay valid across
  Complete() and pending-buffer destruction while recycling uniquely-
  owned buffers across sequential reads via StagingBufferPool.
- Release FilterSourceStream::output_buffer_ on synchronous Read()
  completions so compressed streams also recycle StagingBuffer.
- Track response dispatch in URLLoader so caller-provided body streams
  still run MIME sniffing before committing bytes.
- Add pure virtual DataPipeProducer::DataSource::MayInspectReadBuffer()
  (returning true in FilteredDataSource when a filter is present) so
  DataPipeProducer reads into an intermediate heap buffer before copying
  into the pipe (gated by kStageDataPipeProducerReads), and pass 0 to
  EndWriteData() when Read() fails.

Bug: 579207085

Test: net_unittests --gtest_filter=*FilterSourceStream*
Test: services_unittests --gtest_filter=NetAdaptersTest.*
Test: services_unittests --gtest_filter=URLLoaderTest.*

e15b646c34157aef7acaa54825c3c97fd69c24de [ diff ] 
[workers] Reject redirects from all local-scheme worker main scripts

WorkerScriptFetcher::DetermineFinalResponseUrl() returns the initial
URL for any local-scheme request (`about:`, `blob:`, `data:`,
`filesystem:`), assuming local-scheme worker main-script requests
cannot redirect. Previously, WorkerScriptLoader::OnReceiveRedirect()
only rejected redirects from `blob:` URLs, allowing `about:` and
`filesystem:` worker script requests to follow redirects and bypass
CanCommitURL() on the final response URL.

Extend local-scheme redirect rejection and response metadata
sanitization to all `SchemeIsLocal()` URLs in both
WorkerScriptLoader and WorkerScriptFetcher::OnReceiveRedirect()
(catching throttle-synthesized WillStartRequest redirects before
WorkerScriptLoader is created), guarded by
`kWorkerScriptLocalSchemeRedirectCheck`.

Fixed: 587130425

2851865830a4484702ae08c5b579f24435d2a220 [ diff ] 
[iOS][APC] Don't clip descendants to body when its overflow propagates

When `<html>` keeps `overflow: visible`, CSS moves `body`'s `overflow`
to the viewport, and `body`'s own box clips nothing. Computed styles
still report the value on `body`, so actionable extraction treated
`body` as a clipping container. On pages with a viewport-tall `body`
(e.g. flexispot.com's mobile layout, which sets
`body.style.overflow = "auto"`), scrolling past one screen moved
`body`'s box above the viewport and every in-flow node lost its visible
box and z-order.

The root `addNodeGeometry()` call now skips `body`'s clip in that case,
following Blink's `Document::ViewportDefiningElement()` (an `HTML`/`BODY`
root pair, visible `<html>` overflow, and no `contain` or size
`container-type` on either). Where WebKit differs from Blink, the check
follows WebKit, as confirmed on the simulator: only `<html>`'s
`overflow-x` matters, and `content-visibility: auto` blocks propagation
on `<html>` but not on `<body>`.

Effects, all in actionable mode only:
- In-flow nodes that sit outside `body`'s box but inside the viewport
  get visible boxes, z-order, redaction boxes, and, for cross-site
  iframes, `IframeInfo`.

Before: https://screenshot.corp.google.com/7uvsq41e5obq8
After: https://screenshot.corp.google.com/0lcpd5nt55vfg


NO_IFTTT=Blink already handles body overflow propagation via Document::ViewportDefiningElement().

Bug: 585162852

checkdeps: Fix non-ASCII paths, nested extra repos, trailing separator

Make four edge cases in builddeps.py work. These are for completeness:
none of them changes a result on today's tree.
- git ls-files C-quotes non-ASCII paths, so such a path produced a bogus
  directory name. Use -z, which returns paths unquoted. Today only four
  tracked files have non-ASCII names, all .txt test data in a directory
  that is still checked through its other files.
- An --extra-repos entry below a directory with no tracked files of its
  own was not reached from the base directory. Nothing in chromium/src
  passes --extra-repos.
- A start directory with a trailing separator was not checked unless it
  was the repository root. Only library callers can pass one, as the
  command line normalizes it, and none in the tree does.
- DEPS files were read in the host locale's encoding instead of UTF-8.
  All DEPS files in the tree are ASCII.

Split out of the directory discovery speed-up, which follows and
depends on the trailing separator fix.

Bug: 588284256, 40141077

Exempt --force-renderer-accessibility from auto-disable guard

When only UiAutomation is connected (no AccessibilityService or password
manager enabled), the auto-disable guard in
WebContentsAccessibilityImpl#getAccessibilityNodeProviderCompat() prevents
initializing renderer accessibility on auto-disable candidates.

Exempt --force-renderer-accessibility (unless
--disable-renderer-accessibility is also set) from this guard and from
scheduling the auto-disable timer so instrumentation/UiAutomation tests
passing the switch still expose the web accessibility tree. Also
generate org.chromium.ui.accessibility.AccessibilitySwitches via
java_cpp_strings so Java and C++ share the switch definitions.

Test: out/Android/bin/run_content_shell_test_apk -f "*WebContentsAccessibilityTest#testGetAccessibilityNodeProvider*"
Test: atest desktop-pnp-video-playback-vp9-1080p-30fps -v -- --enable-module-dynamic-download (with ChromePublic.apk / org.chromium.chrome and --force-renderer-accessibility=basic; test config & VideoPlaybackTest locally pointed from com.android.chrome to org.chromium.chrome)
AX-Relnotes: no user-visible change.

289adaa37a7b1002148547fcea88dfb7bc07c530 [ diff ] 
Shut down UpdateEngineClient in OsUpdatesReporterTest::TearDown()

All four tests in OsUpdatesReporterTest called
ash::UpdateEngineClient::InitializeFakeForTest() in their test bodies
without ever calling ash::UpdateEngineClient::Shutdown(). When multiple
OsUpdatesReporterTest cases (or PowerwashTest cases) ran in the same
test batch, the second initialization hit `Check failed: !g_instance`
in UpdateEngineClient::UpdateEngineClient().

Move ash::UpdateEngineClient::InitializeFakeForTest() into
OsUpdatesReporterTest::SetUp() (storing `fake_update_engine_client_` as
a raw_ptr member) and shut it down in OsUpdatesReporterTest::TearDown(),
matching PowerwashTest in the same file.

Bug: 590690997
Fixed: 590690997

7b533376028433ddde6e6ce83a7d68f32bf0cd7a [ diff ] 
[iOS][Actor] Add Autofill annotations to tab observations

Moves actor tab observations closer to desktop actor and iOS Gemini:
- Use default rich extraction, which also extracts paid content markers.
  Child frames were already grafted into the tree.
- Extract Autofill field annotations, which desktop always sends.
- Use the same config in the actor EG test helper so EG tests exercise
  the production config.

Cross-site frames stay excluded on the client. As before this change,
they are redacted in the APC and recorded in
`ScreenshotInfo.iframe_info`, so the server blacks them out in the
screenshot. Including them like desktop needs `iframe_info` populated
for all grafted iframes first (crbug.com/590593880).

parent
82cb89031821359672b63931cbaa6482b084ba4c [ diff ] 
extensions: Skip InstallVerifier bootstrap when verification is off

InstallVerifier::MaybeBootstrapSelf() guarded its null-signature check
with ShouldFetchSignature(), but when signature fetching is disabled
(the default on ChromeOS, Linux, Android, and unbranded builds),
signature_ is always null so IsKnownId() returns false for every
installed webstore extension. That fell into AddMany() and triggered a
redundant synchronous CheckManagementPolicy() pass on startup right
after ExtensionService::Init() had already run it.

Guard MaybeBootstrapSelf() on ShouldFetchSignature() and simplify the
null-signature check.

Bug: b:575251940
Test: unit_tests --gtest_filter=InstallVerifierTest.*

e0da0af73d94a11fb1ee9b9523e4d3d14aed1f67 [ diff ] 
Set url_ in SVGResourceDocumentContent::UpdateDocument()

Restore `url_ = request_url;` in
`SVGResourceDocumentContent::UpdateDocument()`. This assignment was
accidentally dropped in https://crrev.com/c/5458161 when
`IsolatedSVGDocumentHost` was introduced, leaving
`SVGResourceDocumentContent::Url()` empty and causing
`SVGUseElement::UpdateTargetReference()` to miss the fragment-only early
return and can unnecessarily re-fetch external SVG resources.

Bug: 40134477, 585348302
